public enum BlazorECharts.ChartState : int
  Disposed = 5
  Disposing = 4
  Failed = 3
  Initializing = 1
  NotInitialized = 0
  Ready = 2

public abstract class BlazorECharts.Events.ChartEventPayload
  JsonElement NativeReferences { get; }
  JsonElement Raw { get; }
  protected EChartsValue? JsonValue(string name)
  protected IReadOnlyDictionary<string, bool>? BooleanMap(string name)
  protected T? ReadField<T>(string name, Func<JsonElement, T> read) where T : class
  protected T? ReadValue<T>(string name, Func<JsonElement, T> read) where T : struct
  protected bool? Boolean(string name)
  protected ctor(JsonElement json)
  protected double? Number(string name)
  protected int? Integer(string name)
  protected static T[] ReadItems<T>(JsonElement json, Func<JsonElement, T> read)
  protected string? Text(string name)
  void Write(Utf8JsonWriter writer)
